--- FILE: tests/unit/repo/classic/test_classic_repo_adverts.py ---
import pytest
import allure
from uuid import uuid4
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError

from repositories.advert_repository import AdvertsRepository
from sql_builders.advert_sql_builder import AdvertsSqlBuilder

from tests.test_data.models.mother_auth import UserMother
from tests.test_data.models.mother_category import CategoryMother
from tests.test_data.models.mother_adverts import AdvertMother


@allure.feature("Advert Repository")
@allure.story("Unit tests")
class TestAdvertRepositoryUnit:
    """
    Unit тесты репозитория объявлений.

    Договорённость проекта:
        profiles.id == sellers.id == customers.id == user.id (один UUID).

    Следствия для тестов:
        * advert.id_seller == user.id  (а не отдельный seller_id).
        * repo.get_advert_by_user(user.id) возвращает advert'ы, где
          seller.profile_id == user.id, т.е. фактически advert.id_seller == user.id.
        * repo.delete_advert(advert_id, user.id) удаляет advert,
          потому что builder.delete делает WHERE id_seller = :user_id.
    """

    @pytest.fixture
    async def repo(self, test_db_session):
        with allure.step("Создать AdvertsRepository с AdvertsSqlBuilder"):
            return AdvertsRepository(
                session=test_db_session,
                builder=AdvertsSqlBuilder(),
            )

    @pytest.fixture
    async def seeded_user_category(self, test_db_session):
        """
        Готовит в БД профиль, seller'а и категорию.
        Все три записи используют ОДИН UUID (user.id).
        Возвращает (user, category).
        """
        with allure.step("Подготовить профиль, seller и категорию в БД"):
            user = UserMother.valid_user()
            category = CategoryMother.any_category()
            user_id_str = str(user.id)

            # profiles
            await test_db_session.execute(
                text("""
                    INSERT INTO adv_uuid.profiles
                        (id, nickname, fio, email, phone_number, password)
                    VALUES (:id, :nickname, :fio, :email, :phone_number, :password)
                """),
                {
                    "id": user_id_str,
                    "nickname": user.nickname,
                    "fio": user.fio,
                    "email": user.email,
                    "phone_number": user.phone_number,
                    "password": user.password,
                },
            )

            # sellers — тот же id, что и profiles.id
            await test_db_session.execute(
                text("""
                    INSERT INTO adv_uuid.sellers (id, profile_id, rating)
                    VALUES (:id, :profile_id, 0)
                """),
                {"id": user_id_str, "profile_id": user_id_str},
            )

            # categories
            await test_db_session.execute(
                text("INSERT INTO adv_uuid.categories (id, name) VALUES (:id, :name)"),
                {"id": str(category.id), "name": category.name},
            )

            return user, category

    @staticmethod
    async def _create_advert(repo, user, category, **overrides):
        """
        Создаёт advert, привязанный к user'у и категории.
        `user.id` совпадает с `sellers.id` по договорённости,
        поэтому билдер create найдёт seller'а по profile_id = user.id.
        """
        with allure.step("Создать advert для user и category"):
            advert = AdvertMother.valid_advert_for_user_and_category(
                user_id=user.id, category_id=category.id
            )
            for field, value in overrides.items():
                setattr(advert, field, value)
            return await repo.create(advert)

    # =========================================================================
    # 1. create() + get_by_id()
    # =========================================================================
    @allure.story("create()")
    @allure.title("Успешное создание advert")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.CRITICAL)
    async def test_create_advert_success(self, repo, seeded_user_category):
        user, category = seeded_user_category
        with allure.step("Создать advert через репозиторий"):
            advert = AdvertMother.valid_advert_for_user_and_category(
                user_id=user.id, category_id=category.id
            )
            created = await repo.create(advert)

        with allure.step("Проверить результат"):
            assert created is not None
            assert created.content == advert.content

    @allure.story("get_by_id()")
    @allure.title("get_by_id возвращает созданный advert")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.CRITICAL)
    async def test_get_by_id_returns_created_advert(self, repo, seeded_user_category):
        user, category = seeded_user_category
        created = await self._create_advert(repo, user, category)

        with allure.step(f"Получить advert по id={created.id}"):
            fetched = await repo.get_by_id(created.id)

        with allure.step("Проверить поля"):
            assert fetched.id == created.id
            assert fetched.content == created.content

    @allure.story("create()")
    @allure.title("Создание advert с отрицательной ценой → IntegrityError")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.NORMAL)
    async def test_create_advert_with_negative_price_raises_integrity_error(
        self, repo, seeded_user_category
    ):
        user, category = seeded_user_category
        advert = AdvertMother.valid_advert_for_user_and_category(
            user_id=user.id, category_id=category.id
        )
        advert.price = -100

        with allure.step("Ожидать IntegrityError при создании"):
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                await repo.create(advert)

    @allure.story("get_by_id()")
    @allure.title("get_by_id для несуществующего id → ValueError")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.NORMAL)
    async def test_get_by_id_not_found_raises_value_error(self, repo):
        with allure.step("Запросить несуществующий advert"):
            with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="not found"):
                await repo.get_by_id(uuid4())

    # =========================================================================
    # 2. get_all_adverts()
    # =========================================================================
    @allure.story("get_all_adverts()")
    @allure.title("get_all_adverts возвращает созданный advert")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.NORMAL)
    async def test_get_all_adverts_returns_created(self, repo, seeded_user_category):
        user, category = seeded_user_category
        await self._create_advert(repo, user, category)

        with allure.step("Запросить все advert'ы"):
            result = await repo.get_all_adverts()

        with allure.step("Проверить, что созданный присутствует"):
            assert len(result) >= 1

    # =========================================================================
    # 3. get_advert_by_user()
    # =========================================================================
    @allure.story("get_advert_by_user()")
    @allure.title("get_advert_by_user возвращает только advert'ы пользователя")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.CRITICAL)
    async def test_get_advert_by_user_returns_only_his_adverts(
        self, repo, seeded_user_category
    ):
        user, category = seeded_user_category
        created = await self._create_advert(repo, user, category)

        with allure.step(f"Запросить advert'ы пользователя {user.id}"):
            user_adverts = await repo.get_advert_by_user(user.id)

        with allure.step("Проверить выборку"):
            assert len(user_adverts) == 1
            assert user_adverts[0].id == created.id
            assert user_adverts[0].id_seller == user.id  # один UUID на всё

    # =========================================================================
    # 4. is_created()
    # =========================================================================
    @allure.story("is_created()")
    @allure.title("is_created → True для владельца")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.NORMAL)
    async def test_is_created_returns_true_for_owner(self, repo, seeded_user_category):
        user, category = seeded_user_category
        created = await self._create_advert(repo, user, category)

        with allure.step("Проверить is_created для владельца"):
            result = await repo.is_created(user.id, created.id)

        assert result is True

    @allure.story("is_created()")
    @allure.title("is_created → False для чужого пользователя")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.NORMAL)
    async def test_is_created_returns_false_for_stranger(
        self, repo, seeded_user_category
    ):
        user, category = seeded_user_category
        created = await self._create_advert(repo, user, category)

        with allure.step("Проверить is_created для постороннего uuid"):
            result = await repo.is_created(uuid4(), created.id)

        assert result is False

    # =========================================================================
    # 5. Фильтрация
    # =========================================================================
    @allure.story("Фильтрация")
    @allure.title("Поиск по ключевому слову находит advert")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.NORMAL)
    async def test_get_adverts_by_keyword(self, repo, seeded_user_category):
        user, category = seeded_user_category
        created = await self._create_advert(
            repo, user, category, content="Уникальный Игровой Ноутбук"
        )

        with allure.step("Искать advert'ы по ключевому слову 'Игровой'"):
            result = await repo.get_adverts_by_key_word("Игровой")

        with allure.step("Проверить результат"):
            assert len(result) == 1
            assert result[0].id == created.id

    @allure.story("Фильтрация")
    @allure.title("Фильтр по категории находит advert")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.NORMAL)
    async def test_get_adverts_by_category(self, repo, seeded_user_category):
        user, category = seeded_user_category
        created = await self._create_advert(repo, user, category)

        with allure.step(f"Искать advert'ы по категории {category.id}"):
            result = await repo.get_adverts_by_category(category.id)

        with allure.step("Проверить результат"):
            assert len(result) == 1
            assert result[0].id == created.id

    @allure.story("Фильтрация")
    @allure.title("Фильтр по дате включает созданный advert")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.NORMAL)
    async def test_get_adverts_by_date_filter_includes_created(
        self, repo, seeded_user_category
    ):
        user, category = seeded_user_category
        created = await self._create_advert(repo, user, category)

        now = datetime.now()
        with allure.step("Запросить advert'ы за последние сутки"):
            result = await repo.get_adverts_by_filter(
                now - timedelta(days=1), now + timedelta(days=1)
            )

        with allure.step("Проверить результат"):
            assert len(result) == 1
            assert result[0].id == created.id

    # =========================================================================
    # 6. update_advert()
    # =========================================================================
    @allure.story("update_advert()")
    @allure.title("update_advert меняет content и price")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.CRITICAL)
    async def test_update_advert_changes_content_and_price(
        self, repo, seeded_user_category
    ):
        user, category = seeded_user_category
        created = await self._create_advert(repo, user, category)

        created.content = "Полностью Новое Имя"
        created.price = 9999

        with allure.step("Выполнить полное обновление advert"):
            updated = await repo.update_advert(created.id, created)

        with allure.step("Проверить изменения"):
            assert updated.content == "Полностью Новое Имя"
            assert updated.price == 9999

    @allure.story("update_advert()")
    @allure.title("update_advert для несуществующего id → ValueError")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.NORMAL)
    async def test_update_advert_not_found_raises_value_error(self, repo):
        with allure.step("Обновить несуществующий advert"):
            with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="not found"):
                await repo.update_advert(uuid4(), AdvertMother.valid_advert())

    # =========================================================================
    # 7. partial_update_advert()
    # =========================================================================
    @allure.story("partial_update_advert()")
    @allure.title("partial_update_advert меняет только price")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.NORMAL)
    async def test_partial_update_advert_changes_only_price(
        self, repo, seeded_user_category
    ):
        user, category = seeded_user_category
        created = await self._create_advert(repo, user, category)
        original_content = created.content

        with allure.step("Частично обновить только price"):
            updated = await repo.partial_update_advert(created.id, {"price": 12345})

        with allure.step("Проверить, что content не изменился"):
            assert updated.price == 12345
            assert updated.content == original_content

    @allure.story("partial_update_advert()")
    @allure.title("partial_update_advert для несуществующего id → ValueError")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.NORMAL)
    async def test_partial_update_advert_not_found_raises_value_error(self, repo):
        with allure.step("Частично обновить несуществующий advert"):
            with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="not found"):
                await repo.partial_update_advert(uuid4(), {"price": 100})

    # =========================================================================
    # 8. delete_advert()
    # =========================================================================
    @allure.story("delete_advert()")
    @allure.title("delete_advert удаляет advert из БД")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.CRITICAL)
    async def test_delete_advert_removes_it_from_db(self, repo, seeded_user_category):
        user, category = seeded_user_category
        created = await self._create_advert(repo, user, category)

        with allure.step("Удалить advert владельцем"):
            await repo.delete_advert(created.id, user.id)

        with allure.step("Проверить, что advert больше не найден"):
            with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="not found"):
                await repo.get_by_id(created.id)

--- FILE: tests/unit/repo/classic/test_classic_repo_deals.py ---
"""
Unit (интеграционные с БД) тесты репозитория сделок.

Договорённость проекта:
    profiles.id == customers.id == sellers.id == user.id.
    То есть id_customer в deals == user_id (тот же UUID).

Следствия:
    * Чтобы создать deal, нужны: profile + customer с тем же id,
      категория + seller + advert (advert привязан к seller).
    * repo.get_deals_by_user(user.id) вернёт advert'ы,
      у которых в deals.id_customer записан customer с profile_id == user.id.
"""
import uuid

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.exc import SQLAlchemyError

from repositories.deal_repository import DealRepository
from repositories.advert_repository import AdvertsRepository
from sql_builders.deal_sql_builder import DealSqlBuilder
from sql_builders.advert_sql_builder import AdvertsSqlBuilder

from tests.test_data.models.mother_auth import UserMother
from tests.test_data.models.mother_category import CategoryMother
from tests.test_data.models.mother_adverts import AdvertMother


class TestDealRepositoryUnit:

    @pytest.fixture
    async def repo(self, test_db_session):
        return DealRepository(
            session=test_db_session,
            builder=DealSqlBuilder(),
        )

    @pytest.fixture
    async def advert_repo(self, test_db_session):
        return AdvertsRepository(
            session=test_db_session,
            builder=AdvertsSqlBuilder(),
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Вспомогательная функция: создаёт пользователя (profile + customer + seller)
    # с одним и тем же UUID и возвращает объект User.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    async def _seed_user(session, user):
        uid = str(user.id)
        await session.execute(
            text("""
                INSERT INTO adv_uuid.profiles
                    (id, nickname, fio, email, phone_number, password)
                VALUES (:id, :nickname, :fio, :email, :phone_number, :password)
            """),
            {
                "id": uid,
                "nickname": user.nickname,
                "fio": user.fio,
                "email": user.email,
                "phone_number": user.phone_number,
                "password": user.password,
            },
        )
        await session.execute(
            text("""
                INSERT INTO adv_uuid.customers (id, profile_id, rating)
                VALUES (:id, :profile_id, 0)
            """),
            {"id": uid, "profile_id": uid},
        )
        await session.execute(
            text("""
                INSERT INTO adv_uuid.sellers (id, profile_id, rating)
                VALUES (:id, :profile_id, 0)
            """),
            {"id": uid, "profile_id": uid},
        )
        return user

    @staticmethod
    async def _seed_category(session, category):
        await session.execute(
            text("INSERT INTO adv_uuid.categories (id, name) VALUES (:id, :name)"),
            {"id": str(category.id), "name": category.name},
        )
        return category

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Композитная фикстура: user + category + advert (от этого user'а).
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @pytest.fixture
    async def seeded_user_category_advert(self, test_db_session, advert_repo):
        user = UserMother.valid_user()
        category = CategoryMother.any_category()

        await self._seed_user(test_db_session, user)
        await self._seed_category(test_db_session, category)

        advert = AdvertMother.valid_advert_for_user_and_category(
            user_id=user.id, category_id=category.id
        )
        advert = await advert_repo.create(advert)

        return user, category, advert

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Фикстура второго пользователя (для тестов изоляции) + его advert.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @pytest.fixture
    async def seeded_second_user_advert(
        self, test_db_session, advert_repo, seeded_user_category_advert
    ):
        _, category, _ = seeded_user_category_advert
        other_user = UserMother.valid_user()
        await self._seed_user(test_db_session, other_user)

        advert = AdvertMother.valid_advert_for_user_and_category(
            user_id=other_user.id, category_id=category.id
        )
        advert = await advert_repo.create(advert)
        return other_user, advert

    # =====================================================================
    # 1. create_deal()
    # =====================================================================


    async def test_create_deal_when_customer_not_found_raises_sqlalchemy_error(
        self, repo, seeded_user_category_advert
    ):
        _, _, advert = seeded_user_category_advert
        stranger_id = uuid.uuid4()  # в customers такого нет

        with pytest.raises(SQLAlchemyError):
            await repo.create_deal(stranger_id, advert.id)


    async def test_get_deals_by_user_when_no_deals_returns_empty(
        self, repo, seeded_user_category_advert
    ):
        user, _, _ = seeded_user_category_advert

        result = await repo.get_deals_by_user(user.id)

        assert result == []

    # 2.2 У пользователя ровно одна сделка → список из 1 нужного advert.
    async def test_get_deals_by_user_when_one_deal(
        self, repo, seeded_user_category_advert
    ):
        user, _, advert = seeded_user_category_advert
        await repo.create_deal(user.id, advert.id)

        result = await repo.get_deals_by_user(user.id)

        assert len(result) == 1
        assert result[0].id == advert.id

    # 2.3 У пользователя 5 сделок → список из 5, все с нужными id_advert.
    async def test_get_deals_by_user_when_many_deals(
        self, repo, advert_repo, test_db_session, seeded_user_category_advert
    ):
        user, category, _ = seeded_user_category_advert

        # Создаём 5 объявлений и 5 сделок
        expected_advert_ids = set()
        for _ in range(5):
            a = AdvertMother.valid_advert_for_user_and_category(
                user_id=user.id, category_id=category.id
            )
            a = await advert_repo.create(a)
            await repo.create_deal(user.id, a.id)
            expected_advert_ids.add(a.id)

        result = await repo.get_deals_by_user(user.id)

        assert len(result) == 5
        assert {a.id for a in result} == expected_advert_ids


    # 3.1 Пользователь купил это объявление → True.
    async def test_is_in_deals_returns_true_for_buyer(
        self, repo, seeded_user_category_advert
    ):
        user, _, advert = seeded_user_category_advert
        await repo.create_deal(user.id, advert.id)

        assert await repo.is_in_deals(user.id, advert.id) is True



    # 3.3 Объявления не существует → False.
    async def test_is_in_deals_returns_false_when_advert_does_not_exist(
        self, repo, seeded_user_category_advert
    ):
        user, _, _ = seeded_user_category_advert
        nonexistent_advert_id = uuid.uuid4()

        assert await repo.is_in_deals(user.id, nonexistent_advert_id) is False


    # 4.1 Объявление куплено (кем угодно) → True.
    async def test_is_bought_returns_true_when_deal_exists(
        self, repo, seeded_user_category_advert
    ):
        user, _, advert = seeded_user_category_advert
        await repo.create_deal(user.id, advert.id)

        assert await repo.is_bought(advert.id) is True

    # 4.2 Объявление не куплено → False.
    async def test_is_bought_returns_false_when_no_deals(
        self, repo, seeded_user_category_advert
    ):
        _, _, advert = seeded_user_category_advert
        # Сделку не создаём

        assert await repo.is_bought(advert.id) is False

    # 4.3 Объявления не существует → False.
    async def test_is_bought_returns_false_when_advert_does_not_exist(self, repo):
        nonexistent_advert_id = uuid.uuid4()

        assert await repo.is_bought(nonexistent_advert_id) is False
--- END FILE ---

--- FILE: tests/unit/repo/classic/test_classic_repo_user.py ---
import pytest
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError

from repositories.user_repository import UserRepository
from sql_builders.user_sql_builder import UserSqlBuilder

from tests.test_data.models.mother_auth import UserMother


class TestUserRepositoryUnit:
    """
    Unit тесты репозитория пользователей.

    Особенности реализации:
        * create() принимает User, но id игнорируется — БД генерирует новый.
        * create() создаёт сразу profile + customer + seller с одним UUID.
        * delete(profile_id) -> bool.
        * find_by_email(email) -> Optional[User].
    """

    @pytest.fixture
    async def repo(self, test_db_session):
        return UserRepository(
            session=test_db_session,
            builder=UserSqlBuilder(),
        )

    # =========================================================================
    # 1. create()
    # =========================================================================
    async def test_create_user_success(self, repo):
        user = UserMother.valid_user()

        created = await repo.create(user)

        assert created is not None
        assert created.email == user.email
        assert created.nickname == user.nickname

    async def test_create_user_persists_in_db(self, repo):
        user = UserMother.with_email("persist@example.com")
        created = await repo.create(user)

        found = await repo.find_by_email("persist@example.com")

        assert found is not None
        assert found.id == created.id

    async def test_create_user_with_duplicate_email_raises_integrity_error(self, repo):
        # Первый
        user1 = UserMother.with_email("dup@example.com")
        await repo.create(user1)

        # Второй: тот же email, но другой nickname (чтобы упало именно по email)
        user2 = UserMother.with_email("dup@example.com").model_copy(
            update={"nickname": "another_nick"}
        )

        with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
            await repo.create(user2)

    async def test_create_user_with_duplicate_nickname_raises_integrity_error(self, repo):
        user1 = UserMother.with_email("first@example.com")
        await repo.create(user1)

        # Второй: тот же nickname, но другой email
        user2 = UserMother.with_email("second@example.com").model_copy(
            update={"nickname": user1.nickname}
        )

        with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
            await repo.create(user2)

    # =========================================================================
    # 2. find_by_email()
    # =========================================================================
    async def test_find_by_email_returns_user_when_exists(self, repo):
        user = UserMother.with_email("found@example.com")
        created = await repo.create(user)

        found = await repo.find_by_email("found@example.com")

        assert found is not None
        assert found.id == created.id
        assert found.email == "found@example.com"

    async def test_find_by_email_returns_none_when_missing(self, repo):
        found = await repo.find_by_email("missing@example.com")

        assert found is None

    # =========================================================================
    # 3. delete()
    # =========================================================================
    async def test_delete_existing_user_returns_true(self, repo):
        user = UserMother.with_email("delete-me@example.com")
        created = await repo.create(user)

        result = await repo.delete(created.id)

        assert result is True
        # И реально исчез из БД:
        assert await repo.find_by_email("delete-me@example.com") is None
--- END FILE ---

--- FILE: tests/unit/service/classic/test_classic_service_adverts.py ---
"""
Классический стиль тестирования AdvertService.

В этом стиле мы используем фейковые репозитории (Fake*Repository), которые
хранят данные в памяти. Это позволяет проверить реальное взаимодействие
сервиса с репозиториями без базы данных.

Фейки наполняются тестовыми данными в Arrange-секции каждого теста.
Используются Object Mother (AdvertMother, UserMother, CategoryMother).
"""
import uuid

import pytest

from errors.error_services import AdvertNotFound, AdvertUserNotOwn, CategoryNotFound, UserNotFound
from api_v1.dto.advert_dto import AdvertUpdatePartialDTO
from tests.test_data.models.mother_adverts import AdvertMother
from tests.test_data.models.mother_category import CategoryMother
from tests.test_data.models.mother_auth import UserMother


# ==============================================================================
# Вспомогательные функции для наполнения фейковых репозиториев
# ==============================================================================

async def _add_category(fake_repo_category, category):
    """Добавляет категорию в фейковый репозиторий напрямую в хранилище."""
    fake_repo_category._storage[category.id] = category
    return category


async def _add_user(fake_repo_user, user):
    """Добавляет пользователя в фейковый репозиторий через его публичный метод."""
    await fake_repo_user.create(user)
    return user


async def _add_adverts(fake_repo_advert, adverts):
    """Добавляет объявления в фейковый репозиторий через его публичный метод."""
    for advert in adverts:
        await fake_repo_advert.create(advert)
    return adverts

class TestCreateAdvertClassic:
    async def test_create_advert_when_category_not_found(self, service_advert_classic):
        # Arrange
        advert = AdvertMother.valid_advert()
        # Категорию в фейк НЕ добавляем

        # Act & Assert
        with pytest.raises(CategoryNotFound):
            await service_advert_classic.create_advert(advert)

        # Проверяем, что объявление не было создано
        all_adverts = await service_advert_classic.repo.get_all_adverts()
        assert all_adverts == []

    async def test_create_advert_when_category_exists(self, service_advert_classic):
        # Arrange
        category = CategoryMother.any_category()
        await _add_category(service_advert_classic.repo_category, category)
        advert = AdvertMother.valid_advert_for_user_and_category(
            user_id=uuid.uuid4(), category_id=category.id
        )

        # Act
        result = await service_advert_classic.create_advert(advert)

        # Assert
        assert result is not None
        assert result.id == advert.id
        assert result.content == advert.content
        # Проверяем, что объявление действительно сохранилось в репозитории
        stored_advert = await service_advert_classic.repo.get_by_id(advert.id)
        assert stored_advert == advert

class TestGetAllAdvertsClassic:
    async def test_get_all_adverts_when_empty(self, service_advert_classic):
        # Arrange
        # Ничего не добавляем

        # Act
        result = await service_advert_classic.get_all_adverts()

        # Assert
        assert result == []

    async def test_get_all_adverts_when_one(self, service_advert_classic):
        # Arrange
        adverts = AdvertMother.create_list(count=1)
        await _add_adverts(service_advert_classic.repo, adverts)

        # Act
        result = await service_advert_classic.get_all_adverts()

        # Assert
        assert len(result) == 1
        assert result[0] == adverts[0]

    async def test_get_all_adverts_when_many(self, service_advert_classic):
        # Arrange
        adverts = AdvertMother.create_list(count=10)
        await _add_adverts(service_advert_classic.repo, adverts)

        # Act
        result = await service_advert_classic.get_all_adverts()

        # Assert
        assert len(result) == 10
        # Проверяем, что все добавленные объявления присутствуют
        result_ids = {a.id for a in result}
        expected_ids = {a.id for a in adverts}
        assert result_ids == expected_ids

            await service_advert_classic.get_advert_by_user(user_id)

    async def test_get_advert_by_user_when_user_has_no_adverts(self, service_advert_classic):
        # Arrange
        user = UserMother.valid_user()
        await _add_user(service_advert_classic.repo_user, user)

        # Act
        result = await service_advert_classic.get_advert_by_user(user.id)

        # Assert
        assert result == []

    async def test_get_advert_by_user_when_user_has_one_advert(self, service_advert_classic):
        # Arrange
        user = UserMother.valid_user()
        await _add_user(service_advert_classic.repo_user, user)
        adverts = AdvertMother.create_list_for_user(user_id=user.id, count=1)
        await _add_adverts(service_advert_classic.repo, adverts)

        # Act
        result = await service_advert_classic.get_advert_by_user(user.id)

        # Assert

        adverts = AdvertMother.create_list_for_user(user_id=user.id, count=5)
        await _add_adverts(service_advert_classic.repo, adverts)

        # Act
        result = await service_advert_classic.get_advert_by_user(user.id)

        # Assert
        assert len(result) == 5
        # Все объявления принадлежат пользователю
        assert all(a.id_seller == user.id for a in result)

class TestIsCreatedClassic:
    async def test_is_created_when_user_not_found(self, service_advert_classic):
        # Arrange
        user_id = uuid.uuid4()
        # Пользователя не добавляем

        # Act & Assert

            await service_advert_classic.is_created(user_id, advert.id)

    async def test_is_created_when_advert_not_found(self, service_advert_classic):
        # Arrange
        user = UserMother.valid_user()
        await _add_user(service_advert_classic.repo_user, user)
        advert_id = uuid.uuid4()
        # Объявление НЕ добавляем

        # Act & Assert
        with pytest.raises(AdvertNotFound):
            await service_advert_classic.is_created(user.id, advert_id)

    async def test_is_created_when_not_owner(self, service_advert_classic):
        # Arrange
        user = UserMother.valid_user()
        await _add_user(service_advert_classic.repo_user, user)
        # Создаем объявление другим пользователем
        other_user_id = uuid.uuid4()
        advert = AdvertMother.valid_advert_for_user_and_category(
            user_id=other_user_id, category_id=uuid.uuid4()
        )
        await _add_adverts(service_advert_classic.repo, [advert])

        # Act
        result = await service_advert_classic.is_created(user.id, advert.id)

        # Assert
        assert result is False

    async def test_is_created_when_owner(self, service_advert_classic):
        # Arrange
        user = UserMother.valid_user()
        await _add_user(service_advert_classic.repo_user, user)
        advert = AdvertMother.valid_advert_for_user_and_category(
            user_id=user.id, category_id=uuid.uuid4()
        )
        await _add_adverts(service_advert_classic.repo, [advert])

        # Act
        result = await service_advert_classic.is_created(user.id, advert.id)

        # Assert
        assert result is True


# ==============================================================================
# Тесты для метода get_adverts_by_key_word
# ==============================================================================
class TestGetAdvertsByKeyWordClassic:
    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
        "keyword, adverts_to_add, expected_count",
        [
            # 1) пустое ключевое слово (пустая строка), объявления есть в бд
            # В FakeAdvertRepository пустая строка содержится в любом content/description,
            # поэтому вернутся все объявления.
            ("", AdvertMother.create_list(count=5), 5),
            # 2) пустое ключевое слово, бд пустая
            ("", [], 0),
            # 3) ключевое слово содержит пробелы, объявления есть, и есть подходящие
            ("  test  ", AdvertMother.create_list_with_keyword("  test  ", count=5), 5),
            # 4) ключевое слово содержит пробелы, объявления есть, но не подходят
            # (все объявления с content "base test content" — подстрока "  test  " не входит)
            ("  test  ", AdvertMother.create_list(count=5), 0),
            # 5) ключевое слово содержит латиницу, подходящих нет
            # AdvertMother.create_list создает content "base test content",
            # который СОДЕРЖИТ "test". Чтобы не было совпадений, возьмем другое слово.
            ("qwerty", AdvertMother.create_list(count=5), 0),
            # 6) ключевое слово содержит латиницу, подходящие есть
            ("test", AdvertMother.create_list(count=5), 5),
        ],
    )
    async def test_get_adverts_by_key_word(
        self, service_advert_classic, keyword, adverts_to_add, expected_count
    ):
        # Arrange
        await _add_adverts(service_advert_classic.repo, adverts_to_add)

        # Act
        result = await service_advert_classic.get_adverts_by_key_word(keyword)

        # Assert
        assert len(result) == expected_count

        await _add_adverts(service_advert_classic.repo, adverts)

# ==============================================================================
class TestUpdateAdvertClassic:
    async def test_update_when_user_not_found(self, service_advert_classic):
        # Arrange
        user_id = uuid.uuid4()
        advert_data = AdvertMother.valid_advert()
        # Пользователя не добавляем
        advert = AdvertMother.valid_advert()
        await _add_adverts(service_advert_classic.repo, [advert])

        # Act & Assert
        with pytest.raises(UserNotFound):
            await service_advert_classic.update_advert(user_id, advert.id, advert_data)

    async def test_update_when_advert_not_found(self, service_advert_classic):
        # Arrange
        user = UserMother.valid_user()
        await _add_user(service_advert_classic.repo_user, user)
        advert_id = uuid.uuid4()
        advert_data = AdvertMother.valid_advert()
        # Объявление не добавляем

        # Act & Assert
        with pytest.raises(AdvertNotFound):
            await service_advert_classic.update_advert(user.id, advert_id, advert_data)

    async def test_update_when_user_not_owner(self, service_advert_classic):
        # Arrange
        user = UserMother.valid_user()
        await _add_user(service_advert_classic.repo_user, user)
        # Объявление принадлежит другому пользователю
        other_user_id = uuid.uuid4()
        advert = AdvertMother.valid_advert_for_user_and_category(
            user_id=other_user_id, category_id=uuid.uuid4()
        )
        await _add_adverts(service_advert_classic.repo, [advert])
        advert_data = AdvertMother.valid_advert()

        # Act & Assert
        with pytest.raises(AdvertUserNotOwn):
            await service_advert_classic.update_advert(user.id, advert.id, advert_data)

    async def test_update_success(self, service_advert_classic):
        # Arrange
        user = UserMother.valid_user()
        await _add_user(service_advert_classic.repo_user, user)
        advert = AdvertMother.valid_advert_for_user_and_category(
            user_id=user.id, category_id=uuid.uuid4()
        )
        await _add_adverts(service_advert_classic.repo, [advert])

        new_advert_data = AdvertMother.with_id(advert.id)
        new_advert_data.id_seller = user.id

        # Act
        result = await service_advert_classic.update_advert(
            user.id, advert.id, new_advert_data
        )

        # Assert
        assert result.id == advert.id
        assert result.content == new_advert_data.content
        assert result.price == new_advert_data.price
        # Проверяем, что данные в репозитории обновились
        stored_advert = await service_advert_classic.repo.get_by_id(advert.id)
        assert stored_advert.content == new_advert_data.content

class TestPartialUpdateAdvertClassic:
    async def test_partial_update_when_user_not_owner(self, service_advert_classic):
        # Arrange
        user = UserMother.valid_user()
        await _add_user(service_advert_classic.repo_user, user)
        other_user_id = uuid.uuid4()
        advert = AdvertMother.valid_advert_for_user_and_category(
            user_id=other_user_id, category_id=uuid.uuid4()
        )
        await _add_adverts(service_advert_classic.repo, [advert])
        update_data = AdvertUpdatePartialDTO(content="new content")

        # Act & Assert
        with pytest.raises(AdvertUserNotOwn):
            await service_advert_classic.partial_update_advert(
                user.id, advert.id, update_data
            )

    async def test_partial_update_success(self, service_advert_classic):
        # Arrange
        user = UserMother.valid_user()
        await _add_user(service_advert_classic.repo_user, user)
        advert = AdvertMother.valid_advert_for_user_and_category(
            user_id=user.id, category_id=uuid.uuid4()
        )
        await _add_adverts(service_advert_classic.repo, [advert])

        new_content = "updated content"
        new_price = 999
        update_data = AdvertUpdatePartialDTO(content=new_content, price=new_price)

        # Act
        result = await service_advert_classic.partial_update_advert(
            user.id, advert.id, update_data
        )

        # Assert
        assert result.id == advert.id
        assert result.content == new_content
        assert result.price == new_price
        # Проверяем, что данные в репозитории обновились
        stored_advert = await service_advert_classic.repo.get_by_id(advert.id)
        assert stored_advert.content == new_content
        assert stored_advert.price == new_price

    async def test_delete_when_user_not_owner(self, service_advert_classic):

        await _add_user(service_advert_classic.repo_user, user)

        advert = AdvertMother.valid_advert_for_user_and_category(
            user_id=user.id, category_id=uuid.uuid4()
        )
        await _add_adverts(service_advert_classic.repo, [advert])

        # Act
        await service_advert_classic.delete_advert(advert.id, user.id)

        # Assert
        stored_advert = await service_advert_classic.repo.get_by_id(advert.id)
        assert stored_advert is None

--- FILE: tests/unit/service/classic/test_classic_service_auth.py ---
import pytest
from core.create_jwt import JWTManager
from tests.test_data.models.mother_auth import UserMother



class TestAuthServiceClassic:

    # =========================================================================
    # register()
    # =========================================================================
    async def test_register_success_classic(self, service_auth_classic):
        # Arrange
        user = UserMother.valid_user()
        reg_dict = UserMother.to_register_dict(user)

        # Act
        result = await service_auth_classic.register(None, reg_dict)

        # Assert
        assert result is not None
        assert result.email == user.email

        # Дополнительная классическая проверка: данные реально ушли в Fake-хранилище
        saved_user = await service_auth_classic.user_repo.find_by_email(
            user.email
        )
        assert saved_user == result

    async def test_register_duplicate_email_throws_exception_classic(
        self, service_auth_classic
    ):
        # Arrange (Класс эквивалентности: Email уже занят)
        user1 = UserMother.with_email("ivan@example.com")
        await service_auth_classic.user_repo.create(user1)  # Сохраняем первого

        user2 = UserMother.with_email("ivan@example.com")
        reg_dict2 = UserMother.to_register_dict(user2)  # Пробуем второго

        # Act & Assert (Тест на Exception)
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="User already exists"):
            await service_auth_classic.register(None, reg_dict2)

    async def test_register_passwords_dont_match_throws_exception_classic(
        self, service_auth_classic
    ):
        # Arrange (Класс эквивалентности: Пароли не совпадают)
        user = UserMother.valid_user()
        reg_dict = UserMother.to_register_dict(
            user, repeat_password="different_password"
        )

        # Act & Assert
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Passwords didn't matched!"):
            await service_auth_classic.register(None, reg_dict)

    # =========================================================================
    # login()
    # =========================================================================
    async def test_login_success_classic(self, service_auth_classic):
        # Arrange
        user = UserMother.valid_user()
        await service_auth_classic.user_repo.create(user)  # Добавляем в Fake БД

        # Act
        token = await service_auth_classic.login(None, user.email, user.password)

        # Assert
        assert token is not None
        payload = JWTManager.decode_token(token)
        assert payload["sub"] == user.email
        assert payload["id"] == str(user.id)

    async def test_login_email_not_found_throws_exception_classic(
        self, service_auth_classic
    ):
        # Arrange — база фейка изначально пустая, емейла "missing@example.com" там нет

        # Act & Assert
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Invalid credentials"):
            await service_auth_classic.login(
                None, "missing@example.com", "any_password"
            )

    async def test_login_wrong_password_throws_exception_classic(
        self, service_auth_classic
    ):
        # Arrange (Класс эквивалентности: Пароль неверен при верном емейле)
        user = UserMother.valid_user()
        await service_auth_classic.user_repo.create(user)

        # Act & Assert
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Invalid credentials"):
            await service_auth_classic.login(
                None, user.email, "wrong_password_123"
            )

    # =========================================================================
    # verify_token()
    # =========================================================================
    def test_verify_token_revoked_throws_exception_classic(
        self, service_auth_classic
    ):
        # Arrange (Класс эквивалентности: Токен лежит в использованных)
        token = "some_token_string"
        service_auth_classic.invalidated_tokens.add(token)

        # Act & Assert
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Token revoked"):
            service_auth_classic.verify_token(token)
--- END FILE ---

import uuid

import pytest
import allure

from errors.error_services import AdvertNotFound, AdvertUserNotOwn, CategoryNotFound, UserNotFound
from api_v1.dto.advert_dto import AdvertUpdatePartialDTO
from tests.test_data.models.mother_adverts import AdvertMother
from tests.test_data.models.mother_category import CategoryMother
from tests.test_data.models.mother_auth import UserMother


# ==============================================================================
# Тесты для метода create_advert
# ==============================================================================
@allure.feature("Advert Service")
@allure.story("create_advert")
class TestCreateAdvert:
    @allure.title("create_advert: категория не найдена → CategoryNotFound")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.CRITICAL)
    async def test_create_advert_when_category_not_found(
        self, service_advert_london, mock_repo_category, mock_repo_advert
    ):
        # Arrange
        with allure.step("Подготовить advert и мок: категории не существует"):
            advert = AdvertMother.valid_advert()
            mock_repo_category.get_name_by_id.return_value = None

        # Act & Assert
        with allure.step("Ожидать CategoryNotFound при создании"):
            with pytest.raises(CategoryNotFound):
                await service_advert_london.create_advert(advert)

        with allure.step("Проверить вызовы зависимостей"):
            mock_repo_category.get_name_by_id.assert_awaited_once_with(advert.id_category)
            mock_repo_advert.create.assert_not_awaited()

    @allure.title("create_advert: категория существует → advert создан")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.CRITICAL)
    async def test_create_advert_when_category_exists(
        self, service_advert_london, mock_repo_category, mock_repo_advert
    ):
        # Arrange
        with allure.step("Подготовить категорию и advert, настроить моки"):
            category = CategoryMother.any_category()
            advert = AdvertMother.valid_advert_for_user_and_category(
                user_id=uuid.uuid4(), category_id=category.id
            )
            expected_advert = advert.model_copy(update={"id": uuid.uuid4()})
            mock_repo_category.get_name_by_id.return_value = category.name
            mock_repo_advert.create.return_value = expected_advert

        # Act
        with allure.step("Вызвать service.create_advert"):
            result = await service_advert_london.create_advert(advert)

        # Assert
        with allure.step("Проверить результат и вызовы"):
            assert result == expected_advert
            mock_repo_category.get_name_by_id.assert_awaited_once_with(advert.id_category)
            mock_repo_advert.create.assert_awaited_once_with(advert)

@allure.feature("Advert Service")
@allure.story("get_advert")
class TestGetAdvert:
    @allure.title("get_advert: advert не найден → None")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.NORMAL)
    async def test_get_advert_when_not_found(self, service_advert_london, mock_repo_advert):
        # Arrange
        with allure.step("Настроить мок: репозиторий бросает ValueError"):
            advert_id = uuid.uuid4()
            mock_repo_advert.get_by_id.side_effect = ValueError("Not found")

        # Act
        with allure.step("Вызвать service.get_advert"):
            result = await service_advert_london.get_advert(advert_id)

        # Assert
        with allure.step("Проверить результат"):
            assert result is None
            mock_repo_advert.get_by_id.assert_awaited_once_with(advert_id)

    @allure.title("get_advert: advert найден")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.NORMAL)
    async def test_get_advert_when_found(self, service_advert_london, mock_repo_advert):
        # Arrange
        with allure.step("Настроить мок: репозиторий возвращает advert"):
            advert = AdvertMother.valid_advert()
            mock_repo_advert.get_by_id.return_value = advert

        # Act
        with allure.step("Вызвать service.get_advert"):
            result = await service_advert_london.get_advert(advert.id)

        # Assert
        with allure.step("Проверить результат"):
            assert result == advert
            mock_repo_advert.get_by_id.assert_awaited_once_with(advert.id)

    ):

@allure.feature("Advert Service")
@allure.story("is_created")
class TestIsCreated:
    @allure.title("is_created: пользователь не найден → UserNotFound")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.CRITICAL)
    async def test_is_created_when_user_not_found(self, service_advert_london, mock_repo_user, mock_repo_advert):
        # Arrange
        with allure.step("Настроить мок: пользователь не существует"):
            user_id, advert_id = uuid.uuid4(), uuid.uuid4()
            mock_repo_user.does_exist.return_value = False

        # Act & Assert
        with allure.step("Ожидать UserNotFound"):
            with pytest.raises(UserNotFound):
                await service_advert_london.is_created(user_id, advert_id)

        with allure.step("Проверить вызовы зависимостей"):
            mock_repo_user.does_exist.assert_awaited_once_with(user_id)
            mock_repo_advert.advert_exists.assert_not_awaited()
            mock_repo_advert.is_created.assert_not_awaited()

    @allure.title("is_created: advert не найден → AdvertNotFound")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.CRITICAL)
    async def test_is_created_when_advert_not_found(self, service_advert_london, mock_repo_user, mock_repo_advert):
        # Arrange
        with allure.step("Настроить моки: пользователь есть, advert отсутствует"):
            user_id, advert_id = uuid.uuid4(), uuid.uuid4()
            mock_repo_user.does_exist.return_value = True
            mock_repo_advert.advert_exists.return_value = False

        # Act & Assert
        with allure.step("Ожидать AdvertNotFound"):
            with pytest.raises(AdvertNotFound):
                await service_advert_london.is_created(user_id, advert_id)

        with allure.step("Проверить вызовы зависимостей"):
            mock_repo_user.does_exist.assert_awaited_once_with(user_id)
            mock_repo_advert.advert_exists.assert_awaited_once_with(advert_id)
            mock_repo_advert.is_created.assert_not_awaited()

    @allure.title("is_created: пользователь не владелец → False")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.NORMAL)
    async def test_is_created_when_not_owner(self, service_advert_london, mock_repo_user, mock_repo_advert):
        # Arrange
        with allure.step("Настроить моки: is_created возвращает False"):
            user_id, advert_id = uuid.uuid4(), uuid.uuid4()
            mock_repo_user.does_exist.return_value = True
            mock_repo_advert.advert_exists.return_value = True
            mock_repo_advert.is_created.return_value = False

        # Act
        with allure.step("Вызвать service.is_created"):
            result = await service_advert_london.is_created(user_id, advert_id)

        # Assert
        with allure.step("Проверить результат и вызовы"):
            assert result is False
            mock_repo_user.does_exist.assert_awaited_once_with(user_id)
            mock_repo_advert.advert_exists.assert_awaited_once_with(advert_id)
            mock_repo_advert.is_created.assert_awaited_once_with(user_id, advert_id)

    @allure.title("is_created: пользователь владелец → True")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.NORMAL)
    async def test_is_created_when_owner(self, service_advert_london, mock_repo_user, mock_repo_advert):
        # Arrange
        with allure.step("Настроить моки: is_created возвращает True"):
            user_id, advert_id = uuid.uuid4(), uuid.uuid4()
            mock_repo_user.does_exist.return_value = True
            mock_repo_advert.advert_exists.return_value = True
            mock_repo_advert.is_created.return_value = True

        # Act
        with allure.step("Вызвать service.is_created"):
            result = await service_advert_london.is_created(user_id, advert_id)

        # Assert
        with allure.step("Проверить результат и вызовы"):
            assert result is True
            mock_repo_user.does_exist.assert_awaited_once_with(user_id)
            mock_repo_advert.advert_exists.assert_awaited_once_with(advert_id)
            mock_repo_advert.is_created.assert_awaited_once_with(user_id, advert_id)

# ==============================================================================
@allure.feature("Advert Service")
@allure.story("get_adverts_by_key_word")
class TestGetAdvertsByKeyWord:
    @allure.title("get_adverts_by_key_word: параметризованный делегирующий тест")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.NORMAL)
    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
        "keyword, adverts_in_db, expected_result",
        [
            ("", AdvertMother.create_list(count=5), AdvertMother.create_list(count=5)),
            ("", [], []),
            ("  test  ", AdvertMother.create_list_with_keyword("  test  ", count=5), AdvertMother.create_list_with_keyword("  test  ", count=5)),
            ("  test  ", AdvertMother.create_list(count=5), []),
            ("test", AdvertMother.create_list(count=5), []),
            ("test", AdvertMother.create_list_with_keyword("test", count=5), AdvertMother.create_list_with_keyword("test", count=5)),
        ],
    )
    async def test_get_adverts_by_key_word(
        self, service_advert_london, mock_repo_advert, keyword, adverts_in_db, expected_result
    ):
        # Arrange
        with allure.step(f"Настроить мок: keyword={keyword!r}"):
            mock_repo_advert.get_adverts_by_key_word.return_value = expected_result

        # Act
        with allure.step("Вызвать service.get_adverts_by_key_word"):
            result = await service_advert_london.get_adverts_by_key_word(keyword)

        # Assert
        with allure.step("Проверить результат и вызовы"):
            assert result == expected_result
            mock_repo_advert.get_adverts_by_key_word.assert_awaited_once_with(keyword)

    @allure.title("get_adverts_by_category_and_keyword: данные есть")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.NORMAL)
    async def test_with_keyword_and_category_when_data_exists(self, service_advert_london, mock_repo_advert):
        # Arrange

            keyword = "test"

--- FILE: tests/unit/service/london/test_london_service_auth.py ---
import pytest
import allure

from tests.test_data.models.mother_auth import UserMother
from core.create_jwt import JWTManager


@allure.feature("Auth Service")
@allure.story("London school unit tests")
class TestAuthServiceLondon:

    # =========================================================================
    # register()
    # =========================================================================
    @allure.story("register()")
    @allure.title("register: успешная регистрация")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.CRITICAL)
    async def test_register_success_london(self, service_auth_london, mock_repo_user):
        # Arrange
        with allure.step("Подготовить пользователя и настроить моки"):
            user = UserMother.valid_user()
            reg_dict = UserMother.to_register_dict(user)
            mock_repo_user.find_by_email.return_value = None
            mock_repo_user.create.return_value = user

        # Act
        with allure.step("Вызвать service.register"):
            result = await service_auth_london.register(None, reg_dict)

        # Assert
        with allure.step("Проверить результат и вызовы"):
            assert result == user
            mock_repo_user.find_by_email.assert_called_once_with(user.email)

    @allure.story("register()")
    @allure.title("register: email уже занят → ValueError")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.CRITICAL)
    async def test_register_duplicate_email_throws_exception_london(self, service_auth_london, mock_repo_user):
        # Arrange (Класс эквивалентности: Email уже занят)
        with allure.step("Настроить мок: репозиторий находит дубликат"):
            user = UserMother.valid_user()
            reg_dict = UserMother.to_register_dict(user)
            mock_repo_user.find_by_email.return_value = user

        # Act & Assert (Тест на Exception по требованию лабы)
        with allure.step("Ожидать ValueError 'User already exists'"):
            with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="User already exists"):
                await service_auth_london.register(None, reg_dict)

    @allure.story("register()")
    @allure.title("register: пароли не совпадают → ValueError")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.NORMAL)
    async def test_register_passwords_dont_match_throws_exception_london(self, service_auth_london, mock_repo_user):
        # Arrange (Класс эквивалентности: Пароли не совпадают)
        with allure.step("Подготовить reg_dict с несовпадающими паролями"):
            user = UserMother.valid_user()
            reg_dict = UserMother.to_register_dict(user, repeat_password="different_password")
            mock_repo_user.find_by_email.return_value = None

        # Act & Assert
        with allure.step("Ожидать ValueError 'Passwords didn't matched!'"):
            with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Passwords didn't matched!"):
                await service_auth_london.register(None, reg_dict)

    # =========================================================================
    # login()
    # =========================================================================
    @allure.story("login()")
    @allure.title("login: успешный вход, токен валиден")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.CRITICAL)
    async def test_login_success_london(self, service_auth_london, mock_repo_user):
        # Arrange
        with allure.step("Настроить мок: пользователь найден"):
            user = UserMother.valid_user()
            mock_repo_user.find_by_email.return_value = user

        # Act
        with allure.step("Вызвать service.login"):
            token = await service_auth_london.login(None, user.email, user.password)

        # Assert
        with allure.step("Проверить токен и его payload"):
            assert token is not None
            payload = JWTManager.decode_token(token)
            assert payload["sub"] == user.email
            assert payload["id"] == str(user.id)

    @allure.story("login()")
    @allure.title("login: email не найден → ValueError")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.CRITICAL)
    async def test_login_email_not_found_throws_exception_london(self, service_auth_london, mock_repo_user):
        # Arrange (Класс эквивалентности: Емейла нет в БД)
        with allure.step("Настроить мок: пользователь не найден"):
            mock_repo_user.find_by_email.return_value = None

        # Act & Assert
        with allure.step("Ожидать ValueError 'Invalid credentials'"):
            with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Invalid credentials"):
                await service_auth_london.login(None, "missing@example.com", "any_password")

    @allure.story("login()")
    @allure.title("login: неверный пароль → ValueError")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.CRITICAL)
    async def test_login_wrong_password_throws_exception_london(self, service_auth_london, mock_repo_user):
        # Arrange (Класс эквивалентности: Пароль неверен при верном емейле)
        with allure.step("Настроить мок: пользователь найден, пароль неверный"):
            user = UserMother.valid_user()
            mock_repo_user.find_by_email.return_value = user

        # Act & Assert
        with allure.step("Ожидать ValueError 'Invalid credentials'"):
            with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Invalid credentials"):
                await service_auth_london.login(None, user.email, "wrong_password_123")

    # =========================================================================
    # verify_token()
    # =========================================================================
    @allure.story("verify_token()")
    @allure.title("verify_token: токен отозван → ValueError")
    @allure.severity(allure.severity_level.NORMAL)
    def test_verify_token_revoked_throws_exception_london(self, service_auth_london):
        # Arrange (Класс эквивалентности: Токен лежит в использованных)
        with allure.step("Добавить токен в invalidated_tokens"):
            token = "some_token_string"
            service_auth_london.invalidated_tokens.add(token)

        # Act & Assert
        with allure.step("Ожидать ValueError 'Token revoked'"):
            with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Token revoked"):
                service_auth_london.verify_token(token)

import uuid

import pytest

from errors.error_services import DealAlreadyBought, DealDoubleBuying
from tests.test_data.models.mother_adverts import AdvertMother
from tests.test_data.models.mother_auth import UserMother
from tests.test_data.models.mother_deals_likes import DealMother


# ==============================================================================
# Тесты для метода create_deal (3 сценария)
# ==============================================================================
class TestCreateDeal:
    async def test_create_deal_when_already_in_user_deals(
        self, service_deal_london, mock_repo_user, mock_repo_advert, mock_repo_deal
    ):
        """Сценарий 1: сделка уже есть в сделках покупателя."""
        # Arrange
        user = UserMother.valid_user()
        advert = AdvertMother.valid_advert()
        mock_repo_user.does_exist.return_value = True
        mock_repo_advert.advert_exists.return_value = True
        mock_repo_deal.is_bought.return_value = False
        mock_repo_deal.is_in_deals.return_value = True

        # Act & Assert
        with pytest.raises(DealDoubleBuying):
            await service_deal_london.create_deal(user.id, advert.id)

    async def test_create_deal_when_not_yet_in_deals(
        self, service_deal_london, mock_repo_user, mock_repo_advert, mock_repo_deal
    ):
        """Сценарий 2: сделки еще нет — создаётся успешно."""
        # Arrange
        user = UserMother.valid_user()
        advert = AdvertMother.valid_advert()
        deal = DealMother.valid_deal_for(
            customer_id=user.id, advert_id=advert.id
        )
        mock_repo_user.does_exist.return_value = True
        mock_repo_advert.advert_exists.return_value = True
        mock_repo_deal.is_bought.return_value = False
        mock_repo_deal.is_in_deals.return_value = False
        mock_repo_deal.create_deal.return_value = deal

        # Act
        result = await service_deal_london.create_deal(user.id, advert.id)

        # Assert
        assert result == deal

    async def test_create_deal_when_already_bought_by_another_user(
        self, service_deal_london, mock_repo_user, mock_repo_advert, mock_repo_deal
    ):
        """Сценарий 3: сделка уже с другим пользователем."""
        # Arrange
        user_id, advert_id = uuid.uuid4(), uuid.uuid4()
        mock_repo_user.does_exist.return_value = True
        mock_repo_advert.advert_exists.return_value = True
        mock_repo_deal.is_bought.return_value = True

        # Act & Assert
        with pytest.raises(DealAlreadyBought):
            await service_deal_london.create_deal(user_id, advert_id)

--- FILE: tests/test_data/models/builder_adverts.py ---
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from models.advert import Advert  

class AdvertBuilder:
    def __init__(self):
        self.id = uuid.uuid4()
        self.content = "base test content"
        self.description = "base test description"
        self.id_category = uuid.uuid4()
        self.price = 500  
        self.id_seller = uuid.uuid4()
        self.date_created = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

 
    def with_id(self, advert_id: uuid.UUID):
        self.id = advert_id
        return self

    def with_price(self, price: int):
        self.price = price
        return self

    def with_description(self, description: str):
        self.description = description
        return self

    def with_seller_id(self, seller_id: uuid.UUID):
        self.id_seller = seller_id
        return self

    def with_category_id(self, category_id: uuid.UUID):
        self.id_category = category_id
        return self

    def build(self) -> Advert:
        return Advert(
            id=self.id,
            content=self.content,
            description=self.description,
            id_category=self.id_category,
            price=self.price,
            id_seller=self.id_seller,
            date_created=self.date_created
        )

--- END FILE ---

import uuid
from models.user import User

class UserBuilder:
    def __init__(self):
        self.id = uuid.uuid4()
        self.nickname = "test_user"
        self.fio = "Иванов Иван Иванович"
        self.email = "ivan@example.com"
        self.phone_number = "+79991112233"
        self.password = "secure_password123"

    def with_id(self, user_id: uuid.UUID):
        self.id = user_id
        return self

    def with_email(self, email: str):
        self.email = email
        return self

    def with_password(self, password: str):
        self.password = password
        return self

    def with_nickname(self, nickname: str):
        self.nickname= nickname
        return self

    def build(self) -> User:
        return User(
            id=self.id,
            nickname=self.nickname,
            fio=self.fio,
            email=self.email,
            phone_number=self.phone_number,
            password=self.password
        )

import uuid
from models.deal import Deal
from models.liked import Liked


class DealBuilder:

    def __init__(self):
        self.id = uuid.uuid4()
        self.id_advert = uuid.uuid4()
        self.id_customer = uuid.uuid4()
        self.address = "online"

    def with_ids(self, customer_id: uuid.UUID, advert_id: uuid.UUID):
        self.id_customer = customer_id
        self.id_advert = advert_id
        return self

    def build(self) -> Deal:
        return Deal(
            id=self.id,
            id_advert=self.id_advert,
            id_customer=self.id_customer,
            address=self.address,
        )


class LikedBuilder:

    def __init__(self):
        self.id = uuid.uuid4()
        self.id_advert = uuid.uuid4()
        self.id_customer = uuid.uuid4()

    def with_ids(self, customer_id: uuid.UUID, advert_id: uuid.UUID):
        self.id_customer = customer_id
        self.id_advert = advert_id
        return self

    def build(self) -> Liked:
        return Liked(
            id=self.id, id_advert=self.id_advert, id_customer=self.id_customer
        )

--- FILE: tests/test_data/models/mother_adverts.py ---
import uuid
from typing import List
from tests.test_data.models.builder_adverts import AdvertBuilder
from models.advert import Advert

class AdvertMother:
    @staticmethod
    def valid_advert() -> Advert:
        return AdvertBuilder().build()

    @staticmethod
    def with_id(advert_id: uuid.UUID) -> Advert:
        return AdvertBuilder().with_id(advert_id).build()


    @staticmethod
    def create_list(count: int) -> List[Advert]:
        return [AdvertBuilder().build() for _ in range(count)]

    @staticmethod
    def create_list_for_user(user_id: uuid.UUID, count: int) -> List[Advert]:
        return [AdvertBuilder().with_seller_id(user_id).build() for _ in range(count)]

    @staticmethod
    def create_list_for_category(category_id: uuid.UUID, count: int) -> List[Advert]:
        return [AdvertBuilder().with_category_id(category_id).build() for _ in range(count)]

    @staticmethod
    def create_list_with_keyword(keyword: str, count: int) -> List[Advert]:
        return [
            AdvertBuilder().with_description(f"Товар с ключевым словом {keyword} и индексом {i}").build()
            for i in range(count)
        ]

    @staticmethod
    def valid_advert_for(user, category) -> Advert:
        """Advert с привязанными seller и category от переданных моделей"""
        return (
            AdvertBuilder()
            .with_seller_id(user.id)
            .with_category_id(category.id)
            .build()
        )

    @staticmethod
    def with_id_for(advert_id: uuid.UUID, user, category) -> Advert:
        return (
            AdvertBuilder()
            .with_id(advert_id)
            .with_seller_id(user.id)
            .with_category_id(category.id)
            .build()
        )

    @staticmethod
    def create_list_for(user, category, count: int) -> List[Advert]:
        return [
            AdvertBuilder()
            .with_seller_id(user.id)
            .with_category_id(category.id)
            .build()
            for _ in range(count)
        ]

    @staticmethod
    def valid_advert_for_user_and_category(user_id, category_id) -> Advert:
        return (
            AdvertBuilder()
            .with_seller_id(user_id)
            .with_category_id(category_id)
            .build()
        )
--- END FILE ---

--- FILE: tests/test_data/models/mother_auth.py ---
from tests.test_data.models.builder_auth import UserBuilder
from models.user import User

class UserMother:
    @staticmethod
    def valid_user() -> User:
        return UserBuilder().build()

    @staticmethod
    def with_email(email: str) -> User:
        return UserBuilder().with_email(email).build()

    @staticmethod
    def with_password(password: str) -> User:
        return UserBuilder().with_password(password).build()

    @staticmethod
    def with_nickname(nickname: str) -> User:
        return UserBuilder().with_nickname(nickname).build()

    @staticmethod
    def to_register_dict(user: User, repeat_password: str = '') -> dict:
        """Превращает объект User в словарь для метода register сервиса"""
        user_dict = user.model_dump()
        user_dict["id"] = str(user.id)
        user_dict["repeat_password"] = repeat_password or user.password
        return user_dict

--- END FILE ---

--- FILE: tests/test_data/models/mother_category.py ---
from tests.test_data.models.builder_category import CategoryBuilder
from models.category import Category


class CategoryMother:

    @staticmethod
    def any_category() -> Category:
        """Возвращает любую готовую валидную категорию"""
        return CategoryBuilder().build()

--- FILE: tests/test_data/db_scripts/create_tables_and_copy_data.sql ---
-- Очищаем схему перед запуском
DROP SCHEMA IF EXISTS adv_uuid CASCADE;
CREATE SCHEMA adv_uuid;

-- Включаем расширение для UUID
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp";

-- Таблица profiles
CREATE TABLE adv_uuid.profiles (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    nickname VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    fio VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    email VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    phone_number VARCHAR(20),
    password VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Таблица categories
CREATE TABLE adv_uuid.categories (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    name VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE
);

-- Таблица customers
CREATE TABLE adv_uuid.customers (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    profile_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES adv_uuid.profiles(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    rating INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Таблица sellers
CREATE TABLE adv_uuid.sellers (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    profile_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES adv_uuid.profiles(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    rating INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Таблица adverts
CREATE TABLE adv_uuid.adverts (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    content TEXT NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    id_category UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES adv_uuid.categories(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    price INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (price >= 0),
    id_seller UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES adv_uuid.sellers(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    date_created TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- Таблица deals
CREATE TABLE adv_uuid.deals (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    id_advert UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES adv_uuid.adverts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    id_customer UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES adv_uuid.customers(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    date_created TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    address TEXT NOT NULL
);

-- Таблица likes
CREATE TABLE adv_uuid.likes (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    id_customer UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES adv_uuid.customers(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    id_advert UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES adv_uuid.adverts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    date_created TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    UNIQUE (id_customer, id_advert)
);

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION adv_uuid.search_adverts(keyword_like TEXT)
RETURNS TABLE (
    id UUID,
    content TEXT,
    description TEXT,
    id_category UUID,
    price INTEGER,
    id_seller UUID,
    date_created TIMESTAMP
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT a.id, a.content, a.description, a.id_category, a.price, a.id_seller, a.date_created
    FROM adv_uuid.adverts a
    WHERE LOWER(a.content) LIKE LOWER(keyword_like)
       OR LOWER(a.description) LIKE LOWER(keyword_like);
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

--- END FILE ---

--- FILE: tests/test_data/fake_repos/fake_repository_adverts.py ---



import uuid
from typing import List, Optional
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from abstract_repositories.iadvert_repository import IAdvertRepository
from models.advert import Advert


class FakeAdvertRepository(IAdvertRepository):
    def __init__(self):
        # Внутреннее хранилище: id (UUID) -> Advert
        self._storage: dict[uuid.UUID, Advert] = {}

    async def create(self, advert: Advert) -> Optional[Advert]:
        # Если ID пустой (UUID по умолчанию), генерируем новый, как это делает Postgres
        if advert.id == uuid.UUID(int=0) or advert.id is None:
            advert.id = uuid.uuid4()

        # Задаем дату создания, если её нет
        if not advert.date_created:
            advert.date_created = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        self._storage[advert.id] = advert
        return advert

    async def get_by_id(self, advert_id: uuid.UUID) -> Optional[Advert]:
        return self._storage.get(advert_id)

    async def get_all_adverts(self) -> List[Advert]:
        # Сортируем по дате создания (убывание), как в твоем SQL-билдере
        return sorted(
            list(self._storage.values()),
            key=lambda x: x.date_created or datetime.min,
            reverse=True
        )

    async def get_advert_by_user(self, user_id: uuid.UUID) -> List[Advert]:
        return sorted(
            [ad for ad in self._storage.values() if ad.id_seller == user_id],
            key=lambda x: x.date_created or datetime.min,
            reverse=True
        )

    async def is_created(self, user_id: uuid.UUID, advert_id: uuid.UUID) -> bool:
        advert = self._storage.get(advert_id)
        return advert is not None and advert.id_seller == user_id

    async def get_adverts_by_key_word(self, key_word: str) -> List[Advert]:
        kw = key_word.lower()
        return [
            ad for ad in self._storage.values()
            if kw in ad.content.lower() or kw in ad.description.lower()
        ]

    async def get_adverts_by_filter(self, begin_time: datetime, end_time: datetime) -> List[Advert]:
        return [
            ad for ad in self._storage.values()
            if ad.date_created and begin_time <= ad.date_created <= end_time
        ]

    async def get_adverts_by_category(self, category_id: uuid.UUID) -> List[Advert]:
        return [ad for ad in self._storage.values() if ad.id_category == category_id]

    async def get_adverts_by_category_and_keyword(self, keyword: str, category_id: uuid.UUID) -> List[Advert]:
        kw = keyword.lower()
        return [
            ad for ad in self._storage.values()
            if ad.id_category == category_id and (kw in ad.content.lower() or kw in ad.description.lower())
        ]

    async def update_advert(self, advert_id: uuid.UUID, advert: Advert) -> Advert:
        if advert_id not in self._storage:
            raise ValueError(f"Advert with id {advert_id} not found")
        self._storage[advert_id] = advert
        return advert

    async def partial_update_advert(self, advert_id: uuid.UUID, update_data: dict) -> Advert:
        if advert_id not in self._storage:
            raise ValueError(f"Advert with id {advert_id} not found")

        advert = self._storage[advert_id]
        advert_dict = advert.model_dump()

        advert_dict.update(update_data)

        updated_advert = Advert(**advert_dict)
        self._storage[advert_id] = updated_advert
        return updated_advert

    async def delete_advert(self, advert_id: uuid.UUID, user_id: uuid.UUID) -> None:
        if advert_id in self._storage:
            del self._storage[advert_id]

    async def advert_exists(self, advert_id: uuid.UUID) -> bool:
        if advert_id in self._storage:
            return True
        else:
            return False
--- END FILE ---

import uuid
from typing import Optional
from abstract_repositories.iuser_repository import IUserRepository
from models.user import User

class FakeUserRepository(IUserRepository):
    def __init__(self):
        self._storage: dict[uuid.UUID, User] = {}

    async def create(self, user: User) -> User:
        self._storage[user.id] = user
        return user

    async def delete(self, profile_id: uuid.UUID) -> bool:
        if profile_id in self._storage:
            del self._storage[profile_id]
            return True
        return False

    async def find_by_email(self, email: str) -> Optional[User]:
        for user in self._storage.values():
            if user.email == email:
                return user
        return None

    async def does_exist(self, profile_id: uuid.UUID) -> bool:
        for user in self._storage.values():
            if user.id == profile_id:
                return True
        return False
